2Sam 24:24 [וַיֹּאמֶר] [הַמֶּלֶךְ] to/toward [אֲרַוְנָה] not [כִּי] [קָנוֹ] [אֶקְנֶה] [מֵאוֹתְךָ] [בִּמְחִיר] and not [אַעֲלֶה] to YHWH [אֱלֹהַי] [עֹלוֹת] [חִנָּם] [וַיִּקֶן] [דָּוִד] [with] [הַגֹּרֶן] and [with/acc] [הַבָּקָר] [בְּכֶֶף] [שְׁקָלִים] [חֲמִשִּׁים] | [KJV:] And the king said unto Araunah, Nay; but I will surely buy it of thee at a price: neither will I offer burnt offerings unto the Lord my God of that which doth cost me nothing. So David bought the threshingfloor and the oxen for fifty shekels of silver.

So the Lord was intreated for the land, and the plague was stayed from Israel.
